USE ACS20215YR
/*************************************************************************************************** ***************************************
/* Create Tables, Types, Stored Procedures, Etc. Necessary For Service Area Energy Burden Analysis * /
/* ACS Variables Table */
/* Data Source: KEDNY/KEDLI Queries Below */
IF OBJECT_ID(N'ACS_Variables') IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
DROP TABLE ACS_Variables
END
CREATE TABLE [dbo].[ACS_Variables](
[VariableYear] [int] NOT NULL,
[Utility] [varchar](max) NOT NULL,
[VariableName] [varchar](max) NOT NULL,
[VariableValue] [float] NOT NULL,
[VariableMOE] [float] NOT NULL,
[PercentMOE] [float] NOT NULL
) ON [PRIMARY] TEXTIMAGE_ON [PRIMARY]
GO
/* PUMAHouseholds2021_B25002 Table */
/* Data Source: https://data.census.gov/cedsci/table?q=B25002&g=0400000US36%247950000&d=ACS%205­Year%20Estimates%20Detailed%
20Tables&tid=ACSDT5Y2021.B25002&tp=true */
IF OBJECT_ID(N'PUMAHouseholds2021_B25002') IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
DROP TABLE PUMAHouseholds2021_B25002
END
CREATE TABLE [dbo].[PUMAHouseholds2021_B25002](
[id] [varchar](max) NOT NULL,

) ON [PRIMARY] TEXTIMAGE_ON [PRIMARY]
GO

INSERT INTO KEDNY_KEDLI_Households
SELECT *
FROM
(
SELECT 
    Hholds.SerialNo,
CASE WHEN PUMA IS NOT NULL THEN PUMA ELSE 0 END 'PUMA',
CASE WHEN WGTP IS NOT NULL THEN WGTP ELSE 0 END 'WGTP',
0 'PctCECONY',
0 'PctKEDLI',
0 'PctKEDNY',
0 'PctTOTAL',
CASE WHEN WGTP IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds',
CASE WHEN WGTP1 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP1 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds01',
CASE WHEN WGTP2 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP2 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds02',
CASE WHEN WGTP3 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP3 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds03',
CASE WHEN WGTP4 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP4 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds04',
CASE WHEN WGTP5 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP5 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds05',
CASE WHEN WGTP6 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP6 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds06',
CASE WHEN WGTP7 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP7 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds07',
CASE WHEN WGTP8 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP8 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds08',
CASE WHEN WGTP9 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP9 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds09',
CASE WHEN WGTP10 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP10 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds10',
CASE WHEN WGTP11 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP11 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds11',
CASE WHEN WGTP12 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP12 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds12',
CASE WHEN WGTP13 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP13 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds13',
CASE WHEN WGTP14 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP14 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds14',
CASE WHEN WGTP15 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP15 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds15',
CASE WHEN WGTP16 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP16 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds16',
CASE WHEN WGTP17 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP17 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds17',
CASE WHEN WGTP18 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP18 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds18',
CASE WHEN WGTP19 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP19 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds19',
    CASE WHEN WGTP20 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WGTP20 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'Hholds20',

24
'FuelCosts80',
CASE WHEN HINCP IS NOT NULL THEN HINCP ELSE 0 END AS 'HINCP',
CASE WHEN ADJINC IS NOT NULL THEN ADJINC ELSE 0 END AS 'ADJINC',
CASE WHEN ADJHSG IS NOT NULL THEN ADJHSG ELSE 0 END AS 'ADJHSG',
CAST (IncomeLimitHEAP AS FLOAT) 'H_Limit',
CAST (IncomeLimit130PctFPL AS FLOAT) '130PctFPL_Limit',
CAST (IncomeLimitFPL AS FLOAT) 'FPL_Limit',
CAST (IncomeLimitEE AS FLOAT) 'EE_Limit',
CASE WHEN ELEFP IS NOT NULL THEN ELEFP ELSE 0 END 'ELEFP',
CASE WHEN GASFP IS NOT NULL THEN GASFP ELSE 0 END 'GASFP',
CASE WHEN FULFP IS NOT NULL THEN FULFP ELSE 0 END 'FULFP',
CASE WHEN HFL IS NOT NULL THEN HFL ELSE '0' END AS 'HeatingFuel',
CASE WHEN FS IS NOT NULL THEN FS ELSE 0 END 'FS',
/* CASE WHEN Hholds.NP IS NOT NULL THEN Hholds.NP ELSE 0 END 'NP', */
CASE WHEN TEN IS NOT NULL THEN TEN ELSE 0 END 'Tenure',
CASE WHEN RMSP IS NOT NULL THEN RMSP ELSE 0 END 'Rooms',
CASE WHEN WGTP IS NOT NULL AND ACCESSINET IS NOT NULL AND ACCESSINET = '3' THEN WGTP ELSE 0 END 'NoInternet',
CASE WHEN WATFP IS NOT NULL THEN WATFP ELSE 0 END 'WATFP',
CASE WHEN WATP IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(WATP AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END AS 'WaterCosts',
CASE WHEN LNGI IS NOT NULL THEN LNGI ELSE 0 END 'LimitedEnglish',
CASE WHEN HHT IS NOT NULL THEN CASE WHEN CHARINDEX(HHT,'~3~6~7~') > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END ELSE 0 END 'FemaleHouseholder',
sq2.MinAge,
sq2.MaxAge,
sq2.Disability,
sq2.SSI,
sq2.Medicaid,
sq2.PA,
sq2.Race,
sq2.Hispanic,
sq2.Nativity
FROM
(
SELECT
SerialNo,
MIN(Age) AS MinAge,
MAX(Age) AS MaxAge,
MAX(Disability) AS Disability,
MAX(SSI) AS SSI,
MAX(Medicaid) AS Medicaid,
MAX(PA) AS PA,
CASE WHEN SUM(WHITE) > 0 AND SUM([NON­WHITE]) = 0 THEN 1 ELSE
CASE WHEN SUM(WHITE) = 0 AND SUM([NON­WHITE]) > 0 THEN 2 ELSE
CASE WHEN SUM(WHITE) > 0 AND SUM([NON­WHITE]) > 0 THEN 3 END END END AS 'RACE',
CASE WHEN SUM(HISPANIC) > 0 AND SUM([NON­HISPANIC]) = 0 THEN 1 ELSE
CASE WHEN SUM(HISPANIC) = 0 AND SUM([NON­HISPANIC]) > 0 THEN 2 ELSE
CASE WHEN SUM(HISPANIC) > 0 AND SUM([NON­HISPANIC]) > 0 THEN 3 END END END AS 'HISPANIC',
CASE WHEN SUM(NATIVEBORN) > 0 AND SUM([NON­NATIVEBORN]) = 0 THEN 1 ELSE
CASE WHEN SUM(NATIVEBORN) = 0 AND SUM([NON­NATIVEBORN]) > 0 THEN 2 ELSE
CASE WHEN SUM(NATIVEBORN) > 0 AND SUM([NON­NATIVEBORN]) > 0 THEN 3 END END END AS 'NATIVITY'
FROM
(
SELECT
persons.SERIALNO 'SerialNo',
CASE WHEN hholds.HINCP IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(persons.AGEP AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END 'Age',
CASE WHEN hholds.HINCP IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(persons.DIS AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END 'Disability',
CASE WHEN hholds.HINCP IS NOT NULL AND persons.SSIP IS NOT NULL AND CAST(persons.SSIP AS FLOAT) >=4 AND CAST(persons.SSIP AS 
FLOAT) <=30000
THEN 1 ELSE 0 END 'SSI',
CASE WHEN hholds.HINCP IS NOT NULL AND persons.HINS4 IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(persons.HINS4 AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END 'Medicaid',
CASE WHEN hholds.HINCP IS NOT NULL AND persons.PAP IS NOT NULL THEN CAST(persons.PAP AS FLOAT) ELSE 0 END 'PA',
CASE WHEN persons.RAC1P IS NOT NULL AND RAC1P = '1' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS 'WHITE',
CASE WHEN persons.RAC1P IS NOT NULL AND RAC1P <> '1' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS 'NON­WHITE',
CASE WHEN persons.HISP IS NOT NULL AND HISP <> '01' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS 'HISPANIC',
CASE WHEN persons.HISP IS NOT NULL AND HISP = '01' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS 'NON­HISPANIC',
CASE WHEN persons.NATIVITY IS NOT NULL AND NATIVITY = '1' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS 'NATIVEBORN',
CASE WHEN persons.NATIVITY IS NOT NULL AND NATIVITY <> '1' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS 'NON­NATIVEBORN'
FROM psam_p36 AS persons
RIGHT JOIN psam_h36 AS hholds
ON (persons.SERIALNO = hholds.SERIALNO)
) sq1
WHERE sq1.SerialNo IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY ROLLUP(sq1.SerialNo)
) sq2
JOIN psam_h36 AS Hholds
ON (Hholds.SERIALNO = sq2.SERIALNO)
Testimony of William D. Yates, CPA, Cases 23-G-0225, 23-G-0226

JOIN Eligibility
ON (Hholds.NP = Eligibility.NP)
WHERE CAST(Hholds.NP AS FLOAT) > 0 AND
(CAST(Hholds.ELEFP AS INT) = 3 OR CAST(Hholds.GASFP AS INT) = 4) AND
sq2.SerialNo IS NOT NULL

SET PctKEDLI =
CASE
   WHEN PUMA = 4114 THEN @PUMA4114PctKEDLI
   WHEN PUMA > 3107 AND PUMA < 3701 THEN 1
   ELSE PctKEDLI
END
/* KEDNY */
UPDATE KEDNY_KEDLI_Households
SET PctKEDNY =
CASE
   WHEN PUMA = 4101 THEN @PUMA4101PctKEDNY
   WHEN PUMA = 4105 THEN @PUMA4105PctKEDNY

   WHEN PUMA = 4106 THEN @PUMA4106PctKEDNY
   WHEN PUMA = 4107 THEN @PUMA4107PctKEDNY

   WHEN PUMA = 4114 THEN @PUMA4114PctKEDNY

   WHEN PUMA > 3810 AND PUMA < 4101 THEN 1

SET PctTOTAL = PctKEDLI + PctKEDNY

FROM KEDNY_KEDLI_Households
WHERE (GASFP = 2 OR GASFP = 4) AND Income <= WGTP * H_Limit
EXEC  dbo.Usp_SDRPctsSummation @ParTVP
